Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in fungi theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 761 and the invariant I(A,B) = 769 mod 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 761 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the fungi structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 769. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
